Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the model object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 181. The moduli space has dimension at most 772 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_181(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 181. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^772(S, F) is trivial. QED.
